                      - generic [ref=e296]:
                        - heading "Build Overview" [level=2] [ref=e300]
                        - generic [ref=e307]:
                          - paragraph [ref=e308]:
                            - text: This build guide assumes you are
                            - strong [ref=e309]: in maps
                            - text: ", if you are not, click"
                            - link "here for the leveling guide" [ref=e310] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - /url: /poe-2/builds/djinn-sorceress-leveling
                          - paragraph [ref=e311]:
                            - text: "New Video Update out:"
                            - link "https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Dmv8PyVVlXU" [ref=e312] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - /url: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Dmv8PyVVlXU
                          - paragraph [ref=e313]:
                            - strong [ref=e314]: I think cold djinn is entirely unworth it with the new buffs unless there is a new tech with ward skills or something, so that tree is not optimized as much as it could be since i am not playing it, so you should swap some nodes around for archon effect.
                          - paragraph [ref=e315]:
                            - strong [ref=e316]: "Build slightly nerfed: Damage for"
                            - generic [ref=e321]: Kelari's Deception
                            - strong [ref=e322]: is capped to 300% more with susbsequent casts, so far it feels pretty much the same to me.
                          - paragraph [ref=e323]:
                            - strong [ref=e324]: This build essentially becomes a 1 button build with no archon cooldown once you get
                            - generic [ref=e329]: Dominion
                            - strong [ref=e330]: (contempt, suffering, isolation, ancient emotions do not work) anoint and you can just keep spamming
                            - generic [ref=e335]: Kelari's Deception
                            - strong [ref=e336]: and kelaris brutality to keep up crit weakness
                          - heading "Massive Buffs for 0.5:" [level=3] [ref=e337]:
                            - strong [ref=e338]: "Massive Buffs for 0.5:"
                          - list [ref=e339]:
                            - listitem [ref=e340]: Fixed a bug that caused the minion damage bonus against non-unique enemies introduced in version 0.3 to function incorrectly. Depending on specific circumstances, this will result in very little change to early minion damage, approximately 25-35% more late-game minion damage against non-unique enemies, and approximately 20-25% more late-game minion damage against unique enemies. This bonus is also no longer factored into the damage numbers displayed for skills of your minions.
                          - list [ref=e341]:
                            - listitem [ref=e342]: Breach tree jewelry can add minion damage mods
                          - list [ref=e343]:
                            - listitem [ref=e344]:
                              - text: This build is largely unaffected by the Energy Shield nerfs compared to other builds due to the
                              - generic [ref=e349]: The Fourth Teaching
                              - text: ascendancy node /
                              - generic [ref=e354]: Navira's Well
                              - text: It will be one of the few ascendancies that energy shield will still be very OP early
                          - list [ref=e355]:
                            - listitem [ref=e356]: All Command Skills now have a 50% movement speed penalty during skill use (previously 70%).
                          - list [ref=e357]:
                            - listitem [ref=e358]: New nodes on the tree with a buff called puppet master - grants 10% skill speed with command, 10% reduced movement speed penalty, 10% increased, minions 2% movespeed, and minions 3% skill speed PER STACK???
                          - list [ref=e359]:
                            - listitem [ref=e360]: New archon of undeath node - 25% MORE minion damage, 200% MORE cooldown recovery rate for command skills
                          - list [ref=e361]:
                            - listitem [ref=e362]: More minion command cooldown nodes on the tree
                          - list [ref=e363]:
                            - listitem [ref=e364]:
                              - generic [ref=e369]: Dominion
                              - text: (secret anoint) can be used for permanent archon
                          - heading "CHECK FOR MORE INFO UNDER EACH SECTION, ctrl + f is a GREAT utility" [level=3] [ref=e370]:
                            - strong [ref=e371]: CHECK FOR MORE INFO UNDER EACH SECTION, ctrl + f is a GREAT utility
                          - heading "What is the difference between djinns?:" [level=3] [ref=e372]:
                            - strong [ref=e373]: "What is the difference between djinns?:"
                          - paragraph [ref=e374]:
                            - generic [ref=e379]: Kelari, the Tainted Sands
                            - text: is by far the best one, having the best DPS and utility applying critical weakness on enemies, AND it is still decent at clearing packs.
                          - paragraph [ref=e380]:
                            - generic [ref=e385]: Ruzhan, the Blazing Sword
                            - text: is the second best,
                            - generic [ref=e390]: Ruzhan's Trap
                            - text: is very nice for supplemental clear and DPS. It can also be used to keep perma 6 stacks on puppet master and trigger undead archon with ruzhans brand
                          - paragraph [ref=e391]:
                            - generic [ref=e396]: Navira's Fracturing
                            - text: is the worst but still good it just requires a 3 button piano combo between
                            - generic [ref=e401]: Frost Bomb
                            - text: ", picking up remnants,"
                            - generic [ref=e406]: Ice Nova
                            - text: and
                            - generic [ref=e411]: Navira's Fracturing
                            - text: which is probably annoying for most people, but satisfying screen wide pops. I don't think it is better in clear speed than the other 2 despite being screen wide AoE due to how long the combo takes to cast. Some people mention using a cold quill crab spectre to automatically apply chilled ground but I didn't have much success with that, maybe there is a secret tech.
                          - heading "Misc Info:" [level=3] [ref=e412]
                          - paragraph [ref=e413]:
                            - strong [ref=e414]: You can disenchant your spectre gem to get a base version back and not waste it.
                          - paragraph [ref=e415]: You can swap to CI around 6k ES
                          - paragraph [ref=e416]: You get additional sockets for your djinns by leveling up - levels that grant additional sockets are 40, 65, and 90.
                          - paragraph [ref=e417]:
                            - text: If you are randomly getting chunked try getting rid of
                            - generic [ref=e422]: Unstable Bond
                            - text: but I haven't had any problem with it
                          - paragraph [ref=e423]: Cold, fire, phys levels on wands / staffs do not level up your djinns
                          - paragraph [ref=e424]: You can use ruzhans brand to have perma 6 stacks puppet master
                          - paragraph [ref=e425]:
                            - text: Get 1 of each minion type for
                            - generic [ref=e430]: Muster
                            - text: then any extra spirit can be for getting more spectres. Once you have 1 of each you can either dump the rest into spectres or get
                            - generic [ref=e435]: Wolf Pack
                            - text: with
                            - generic [ref=e440]: Infernal Legion II
                            - text: /
                            - generic [ref=e445]: Amanamu's Tithe
                            - text: for perma 3 abyss mods and
                            - generic [ref=e450]: Romira's Requital
                          - paragraph [ref=e451]:
                            - link "https://poe.ninja/poe2/profile/dslily-1229/runesofaldur/character/lilydjinnopwtf" [ref=e452] [cursor=pointer]:
                              - /url: https://poe.ninja/poe2/profile/dslily-1229/runesofaldur/character/lilydjinnopwtf
                          - paragraph [ref=e453]: "Pillars: movement speed or global defence"
                          - paragraph [ref=e454]: "Venom Draught: Stun threshold"
                          - paragraph [ref=e455]: "Tattoos: Resistance"
                          - paragraph [ref=e456]: "Shark fin: 30% es"
                          - heading "FAQ:" [level=3] [ref=e457]
                          - paragraph [ref=e458]: "Q: There's too many gems A: Take the less important ones out"
                          - paragraph [ref=e459]: "Q: is there a filter A: no just use filterblade"
                          - paragraph [ref=e460]: "Q: how to fix mana problems? A: press your mana flask or stop holding down your mouse button and just click, holding it down costs more mana than the times it actually casts."
                          - paragraph [ref=e461]: "Q: what dmg stats work? A: Any minion stats work, spell / atk damage on yourself does not work, but cast speed does. I dont think getting cast speed is worth tho"
                          - paragraph [ref=e462]:
                            - text: "Q: when should i start using"
                            - generic [ref=e467]: Dominion
                            - text: "A: when you can afford it"
                          - paragraph [ref=e468]: "Q: My djinn cooldowns are not decreasing when i have archon for a few seconds A: there is a bug where if you keep casting it at 0 charges left it doesnt give you the cooldown recovery"
                          - paragraph [ref=e469]:
                            - text: "Q: Where to get quality? A: you dont or"
                            - generic [ref=e474]: Dialla's Desire
                          - paragraph [ref=e475]:
                            - text: "Q: Why do i take infusions? A:"
                            - generic [ref=e480]: Time-Lost Sapphire

                            - heading "Modifier Priority:" [level=3] [ref=e788]
                            - paragraph [ref=e789]: Get resistance / attributes / rarity wherever you can.
                            - paragraph [ref=e790]:
                              - text: "Sceptre: +minion levels > spirit > ally damage bonuses. For basetype you can go any purity of lightning/cold/fire sceptre or"
                              - generic [ref=e795]: Rattling Sceptre
                              - text: . If you use a purity sceptre then you can use
                              - generic [ref=e800]: Upwelling II
                              - text: /
                              - generic [ref=e805]: Clarity II
                              - text: /
                              - generic [ref=e810]: Cool Headed
                              - text: /
                              - generic [ref=e815]: Strong Hearted
                              - text: /
                              - generic [ref=e820]: Warm Blooded
                              - text: in the sceptre for no spirit cost. Socket
                              - generic [ref=e825]: Primate Idol
                              - text: when you have
                              - generic [ref=e830]: Dominion
                              - text: otherwise socket
                              - generic [ref=e835]: Rune of Renown
                            - paragraph [ref=e836]: "Focus: ES / +minion levels"
                            - paragraph [ref=e837]:
                              - text: "Helmet: % ES and flat ES > archon buff duration (cyclonic alloy) / +minion levels"
                              - generic [ref=e842]: Amanamu's Gaze
                              - text: is extremely good.
                            - paragraph [ref=e843]:
                              - text: "Body armour: % ES and flat ES > Spirit, good budget option is"
                              - generic [ref=e848]: Silks of Veneration
                            - paragraph [ref=e849]: "Gloves: % ES and flat ES"
                            - paragraph [ref=e850]: "Boots: Movement speed > %ES and flat ES"
                            - paragraph [ref=e851]:
                              - text: "Ring:"
                              - generic [ref=e856]: Evergrasping Ring
                              - text: is very good for cheap dmg, can wear 2 for gigadam,
                              - generic [ref=e861]: Ventor's Gamble
                              - text: for rarity
                            - paragraph [ref=e862]:
                              - text: "Belt: Rare with command skill damage > minion aoe > res or"
                              - generic [ref=e867]: Mageblood
                              - text: with quicksilver
                            - paragraph [ref=e868]: "Jewel: ES % / recharge / any minion damage"
                            - paragraph [ref=e869]:
                              - text: "Time lost jewel: upgrade to very large with ancient melancholy, notables give minion crit chance / dmg, If you can't afford a"
                              - generic [ref=e874]: Time-Lost Sapphire

                            - paragraph [ref=e875]:

                              - generic [ref=e881]: Dominion
                              - strong [ref=e882]: (contempt, suffering, isolation, ancient emotions do not work) anoint and you can just keep spamming
                              - generic [ref=e887]: Kelari's Deception
                              - strong [ref=e888]: and kelaris brutality to keep up crit weakness, early can be
                              - generic [ref=e893]: Warlord Leader
                              - strong [ref=e894]: ", can do"

                              - strong [ref=e900]: early for extra AoE, or

                              - strong [ref=e906]: "for some extra defense. Mods:"
